NOTIFICATION

The draft of Karnataka Grama Swaraj and Panchayat Raj (Staffing Pattern, Scale of
Pay, Method of Recruitment and other Conditions of service of Employees of Grama
Panchayat) Rules, 2020, with further amendments, Which the Government of Karnataka
proposes to make, in exercise of the powers conferred by section 311 read with the clause
(d) of the sub-section (1) of section 62 and sections 112 and 113 of the Karnataka Grama
Swaraj and Panchayath Raj Act 1993 (Karnataka Act 14 of 1993) is here by published as
required by section 311 of the said Act for the information of all persons likely to be
affected and hereby objections and suggestion are invited and it is hereby directed to send
objections and suggestions with in fifteen days from the date of publication of these Rules
in the Karnataka Gazette.
Any objections or suggestions which may be received by the State from any person
with regard to this draft before the expiry of the period specified above will be considered
by the State Government. Objections or suggestions may be addressed to the Additional
(15971)

OBE 2S BMF UE TBH, Aemdwod, 02, HHows*, 5099 on Ya
Chief Secretary to Government, (PR) Rural Development and Panchayath Raj Department,
3rd Floor, 3rd gate, MS Building, Dr. B.R. Ambedkar Veedhi, Bengaluru -560 001.

DRAFT RULES
1. Title and Commencement.- (1) These rules may be called the Karnataka Grama
Swaraj and Panchayat Raj (Staffing Pattern, Scale of Pay, Method of Recruitment and other
Conditions of service of Employees of Grama Panchayat) (Amendment) Rules, 2022.
(2) They shall come into force from the date of their final publication in the Official
Gazette.
2. Amendment of Rule 3.- In the Karnataka Grama Swaraj and Panchayat Raj
(Staffing Pattern, Scale of Pay, Method of Recruitment and other Conditions of service of
Employees of Grama Panchayat) Rules, 2020 (hereinafter referred to as the said rules) in rule
3 the words “ Number of posts” shall be omitted.
3. Amendment of Rule 4.- (1) In Rule 4 of the said Rules,-

a. In sub rule 1(b), for the words “Deputy Director, Social welfare Department” the
words “District Social welfare Officer, Social welfare Department” Shall be substituted.
b. after (f) the following shall be inserted, namely:-
(g) concerned Grama Panchayat President
c. In sub rule (4) for the words “invite applications from eligible candidates by
advertising the notice of recruitment and specify the category of post, age limit,
qualification and other details and specifying the last date of filing application, in the
widely circulated news paper of district of which at least one shall be in Kannada.” the
words “Shall invite applications from eligible candidates by advertising the notice of
recruitment specifying the category of post, age limit, qualification and other details
and specifying the last date of filing application, in the widely circulated news paper
of district of which at least one or two shall be in Kannada” Shall be substituted.
4. Amendment of Rule 5.- (1) In sub rule 5(1), for the words “Notified to the
category of posts” the words “Notified for the category of posts” Shall be substituted.
(2) In sub rule 5(5) (d) for the words “Physically handicapped certificates” the words “Persons
with disabilities” Shall be substituted.
3. after (f) Inclusion of name in the select list does not confer any right of appointment
on the candidates the following shall be inserted, namely:-
“District level selection committee shall select the eligible candidates to the vacant posts in
the Grama panchayats and forward the selected list of candidates to the appointing
authority, namely, the Grama Panchayat, concerned. The Appointing Authority shall proceed
to make the Appointment as per the selection list approved by the selection committee
chaired by the Chief Executive Officer of the ZP.”

won 2 BMF UE BDH, Aoemd, 02, Saowo", 9095 eriorace
5. Amendment of Rule 6.- In Rule 6, of the said rules, after proviso, the following shall be
inserted, namely:-
“Where the employees of Gram Panchayats, such as Water Operator/Pump
Operator/Attendant/Cleaners of the Gram Panchayat are promoted to the vacant posts of
Bill collector and / or Clerk Cum Data Entry Operators, who were appointed as per Section
113 of Karnataka Grama Swaraj and Panchayat Raj Act 1993, it is necssary to obtain the
approval of the Chief Executive Officer of the Zilla Panchayat before making the
appointment on promotion."
6. Amendment of Rule 7.- (1) In sub rule 7(ii), for the words “competent authority to
sanction causal leave of 10 days, 2 days restricted holiday and earned leaves up to 30 days
to the employee, in the year” the words “competent authority to sanction casual leave for 10
days, 2 days restricted holiday and earned leaves up to 30 days to the employee, in a year”
Shall be substituted.
(2) In sub rule 7(iii) for the words “fraternity leave” the words “Paternity Leave” Shall be
substituted.
(3) Transfer of Bill Collector and Clerk cum Data Entry Operator:- In special
circumstances with the consent of the concerned bill collector and clerk cum data entry
operator, whose appointment is approved by he Chief Executive Officer, transfer shall be
effected by the chief executive officer of the Zilla Panchayat within the district to the
available vacant posts and mutual transfer shall also be effected without any adverse effect
on their seniority. But all these transfers shall be effected only twice in their tenure of
service.
7. Amendment of Rule 8.- (1) In Rule 8 of the said Rules,-
(1) In sub Rule (a), for the words “or withholding of increment” shall be omitted.
(2) In sub rule 8(3), for the words “Action an inquiry” the words “Action on inquiry” Shall be
substituted.
(3) In sub rule 8(4)(iii) for the words “The recommendation of the Lokayukta is binding on
the Grama Panchayat” the words “The recommendation of the Lokayukta or Anti corruption
Bureau (ACB) is binding on the Grama Panchayat” Shall be omitted.
8. Amendment of Rule 9.- (1) In Rule 9 of the said Rules,-
(1) In Sub Rule (1) for the words “Eligible person of the deceased employee shall
apply for a post within one year from the date of the death to the concerned Grama
Panchayat” the words “Every dependent of a deceased Grama Panchayat Employee seeking
appointment under these rules shall make an application with in one year from the date of
death of the Employee of the Grama Panchayat under whom the deceased Grama
Panchayat Employee was working.
provided that, in case of a minor he must have attained the age of eighteen years
within two years from the date of death of the Grama Panchayat Employee and he must
make an application within two years thereafter.

(2) In sub rule,- for sub rule 2(i) the following shall be substituted namely:-
“(i) in the case of the deceased male married Grama Panchayat Employee,-
a. the widow; and
b. son or daughter who were dependent on him and were living with him. who is
chosen by the widow of the deceased Grama Panchayat Employee, if the widow is not
eligible or she is not willing to accept the appointment for any valid reason”.
Note: In case spouse is pre-deceased then preference shall be given in the order of age
of children.
(2) for clause (ia), the following shall be substituted, namely:-
“(ia) in the case of the deceased male unmarried Grama Panchayat Employee, his brother
or sister, who is chosen by father and mother or in case of any difference in their
opinion, then the person chosen by mother, who were dependent upon him and were
living with him”.
Note:In case parents are pre-deceased then preference shall be given in the order of age
of brother / sister.
(3) for clause (ii), the following shall be substituted namely:-
“(ii) in the case of the deceased female married Grama Panchayat Employee:
a. son or daughter who were dependent on her and were living with her who is chosen
by the widower of the deceased Grama Panchayat Employee; and
b. widower; if the son or daughter are not eligible or for any valid reason they are not
willing to accept the appointment:.
Note: In case spouse is pre-deceased then preference shall be given in the order of age
of children.
(4) for clause (iia), the following shall be substituted, namely:-
“(iia) in the case of the deceased female unmarried Grama Panchayat Employee, her
brother of sister, who is chosen by father and mother or in case of difference in their
opinion, the person chosen by mother, who were dependent upon her and were living
with her”.
Note: In case parents are pre-deceased then preference shall be given in the order of
age of brother / sister.
(5) after clause (iia), the following clause shall be inserted,
“(iii) in the case of deceased married Grama Panchayat Employee whose spouse is also
no more and who has minor children, certified guardian of the minor children who is
living with them and takes care of the minor children as per the provisions of relevant
law;
(iv) the children of the deceased unlawful second wife and thereafter spouse,

9. In the Schedule Serial No. 1, the words and clerk cum Data Entry Operater shall be
omitted, serial number 3,4,5 shall function as multipurpose workers in the Gram
Panchayat and in the serial number 5 the following shall be substituted, namely:-

PRELIMINARY NOTIFICATION
In exercise of the powers conferred by Section 5 of the Karnataka Land Revenue
Act 1964 (Karnataka act no 12 of 1964) and taking into consideration the factors
specified in clauses (i) to (iv) of rule 3 of the Karnataka Land Revenue Rules 1966
such as the population, area of the village the demand under land revenue in the village
and other head of the account and having regard to the administrative convenience the
Government of Karnataka proposes to issue a Notification to alter the limits of
Haranahalli Village of Arasikere taluk Hassan District and to constitute a new
revenue village called Yarehalli, Kankanchenahalli, Hanumanthanagara as
specified in schedule along with maps with old survey numbers and new assigned to
them.
Now, therefore in exercise of the power conferred by section 6 of the said Act,
notice is hereby given for the information of all the persons likely to be affected
thereby that the proposal will be taken into consideration on or after thirty days from
the date of publication of the notification in the official Gazette.

Any objection or suggestion which may be received by the state Government
from any person with respect to the said proposal before the date specified above will
be considered by the state Government. Objections or suggestion may be addressed to
the Deputy Commissioner, Hassan District, Hassan.
SCHEDULE-1
Land bearing survey numbers of the village and survey numbers of the areas
specified in column (2) of the table below shall be excluded from the limits of said
village Haranahalli of Arasikere taluk in Hassan District to form a new revenue
village called Yarehalli of Arasikere taluk Hassan district along with the survey
numbers included in column (4) thereof.

GOVERNMENT OF KARNATAKA
RD 37 Kamgrara 2018 Karnataka government Secretariat
Multi Storied Building
Bengaluru, Date: 05-11-2022

FINAL NOTIFICATION
Whereas the draft of the Notification No RD 37 Kamgrara 2018
Dated:24-03-2018 regarding altering the limits of Chimmegaon Village of
Kamalanagar Taluk, Bidar District and to constitute a new village called Sevalal
Tanda and Dhoparwadi was published as required by section 6 of the Karnataka land
Revenue Act 1964 (Karnataka act 12 of 1964) in the Karnataka Gazette No
RD 37 Kamgrara 2018 Dated:05-07-2018 inviting Objections or suggestions from
all the persons likely to be affected thereby within thirty days from the date of its
publication in the official gazette.

And Where as said the gazette was made available to the public on
Dated:02-12-2021 and where as Objections or suggestions have been received and
considered by the State Government;

Now therefore, in exercise of the powers conferred by section 5 of the
Karnataka land Revenue Act 1964 (Karnataka act 12 of 1964) the Government of
Karnataka hereby makes the following notification ,namely.

SCHEDULE-I

Land bearing survey numbers of the village and survey numbers of the areas
specified in column (2) of the table below shall be excluded from the limits of said
village Chimmegaon of Kamalanagar Taluk, Bidar district to form a new revenue
village called as Sevalal Tanda of Kamalanagar Taluk, Bidar district along with the
survey numbers included in column (4) thereof.

Revenue Act 1964 (Karnataka act 12 of 1964) in the Karnataka Gazette No
RD 735 Kamgrara 2017 Dated:20-11-2017 inviting Objections or suggestions from
all the persons likely to be affected thereby within thirty days from the date of its
publication in the official gazette.
And Where as said the gazette was made available to the public on

considered by the State Government;

Karnataka land Revenue Act 1964 (Karnataka act 12 of 1964) the Government of
Karnataka hereby makes the following notification ,namely.

specified in column (2) of the table below shall be excluded from the limits of said
village Chikanagaon of Basavakalyana Taluk, Bidar district to form a new revenue
village called as Chikanagaonwadi of Basavakalyana Taluk, Bidar district along
with the survey numbers included in column (4) thereof.

GOVERNMENT OF KARNATAKA
RD 711 Kamgrara 2017 Karnataka government Secretariat
Multi Storied Building
Bengaluru, Date: 05-11-2022

FINAL NOTIFICATION
Whereas the draft of the Notification No RD 711 Kamgrara 2017
Dated:04-10-2017 regarding altering the limits of Gadavanti Village of
Humanabad Taluk, Bidar District and to constitute a new village called
Basanthapura was published as required by section 6 of the Karnataka land Revenue
Act 1964 (Karnataka act 12 of 1964) in the Karnataka Gazette No RD 711 Kamgrara
2017 Dated:07-12-2017 inviting Objections or suggestions from all the
persons likely to be affected thereby within thirty days from the date of its publication
in the official gazette.

And Where as said the gazette was made available to the public on
Dated:23-11-2021 and where as Objections or suggestions have been received and
considered by the State Government;

Now therefore, in exercise of the powers conferred by section 5 of the
Karnataka land Revenue Act 1964 (Karnataka act 12 of 1964) the Government of
Karnataka hereby makes the following notification ,namely.

SCHEDULE-I

Land bearing survey numbers of the village and survey numbers of the areas
specified in column (2) of the table below shall be excluded from the limits of said
village Gadavanti of Humanabad Taluk, Bidar district to form a new revenue
village called as Basanthapura of Humanabad Taluk, Bidar district along with the
survey numbers included in column (4) thereof.

PR-1054
GOVERNMENT OF KARNATAKA
RD 36 Kamgrara 2018 Karnataka government Secretariat
Multi Storied Building
Bengaluru, Date:05-11-2022

FINAL NOTIFICATION
Whereas the draft of the Notification No RD 36 Kamgrara 2018
Dated: 09-02-2018 regarding altering the limits of Murki Village of Kamalanagar
Taluk, Bidar District and to constitute a new village called Shivpur and Murkiwadi
was published as required by section 6 of the Karnataka land Revenue Act 1964
(Karnataka act 12 of 1964) in the Karnataka Gazette No RD 36 Kamgrara 2018
Dated:07-06-2018 inviting Objections or suggestions from all the persons likely to
be affected thereby within thirty days from the date of its publication in the official
gazette.

And Where as said the gazette was made available to the public on
Dated:02-12-2021 and where as Objections or suggestions have been received and
considered by the State Government;

Now therefore, in exercise of the powers conferred by section 5 of the
Karnataka land Revenue Act 1964 (Karnataka act 12 of 1964) the Government of
Karnataka hereby makes the following notification ,namely.

SCHEDULE-I

Land bearing survey numbers of the village and survey numbers of the areas
specified in column (2) of the table below shall be excluded from the limits of said
village Murki of Kamalanagar Taluk, Bidar district to form a new revenue village
called as Shivpur of Kamalanagar Taluk, Bidar district along with the survey
numbers included in column (4) thereof.

GOVERNMENT OF KARNATAKA

Bengaluru, Date: 05-11-2022

FINAL NOTIFICATION

Whereas the draft of the Notification No RD 722 Kamgrara 2017
Dated: 04-10-2017 regarding altering the limits of Sonal Village of Kamalanagar
Taluk, Bidar District and to constitute a new village called Sonalwadi was published
as required by section 6 of the Karnataka land Revenue Act 1964 (Karnataka act 12 of
1964) in the Karnataka Gazette No RD 722 Kamgrara 2017 Dated:07-12-2017
inviting Objections or suggestions from all the persons likely to be affected thereby
within thirty days from the date of its publication in the official gazette.

And Where as said the gazette was made available to the public on
Dated:02-12-2021 and where as Objections or suggestions have been received and
considered by the State Government;

Now therefore, in exercise of the powers conferred by section 5 of the
Karnataka land Revenue Act 1964 (Karnataka act 12 of 1964) the Government of
Karnataka hereby makes the following notification ,namely.

SCHEDULE-I
Land bearing survey numbers of the village and survey numbers of the areas
specified in column (2) of the table below shall be excluded from the limits of said

PR-1056
GOVERNMENT OF KARNATAKA
RD 719 Kamgrara 2017 Karnataka government Secretariat
Multi Storied Building
Bengaluru, Date: 05-11-2022

FINAL NOTIFICATION
Whereas the draft of the Notification No RD 719 Kamgrara 2017
Dated: 04-10-2017 regarding altering the limits of Talamadagi Village of
Chitaguppa Taluk, Bidar District and to constitute a new village called Gandhinagar
was published as required by section 6 of the Karnataka land Revenue Act 1964
(Karnataka act 12 of 1964) in the Karnataka Gazette No RD 719 Kamgrara 2017
Dated:07-12-2017 inviting Objections or suggestions from all the persons likely to
be affected thereby within thirty days from the date of its publication in the official
gazette.

And Where as said the gazette was made available to the public on
Dated:25-11-2021 and where as Objections or suggestions have been received and
considered by the State Government;

Now therefore, in exercise of the powers conferred by section 5 of the
Karnataka land Revenue Act 1964 (Karnataka act 12 of 1964) the Government of
Karnataka hereby makes the following notification ,namely.

SCHEDULE-I

Land bearing survey numbers of the village and survey numbers of the areas
specified in column (2) of the table below shall be excluded from the limits of said
village Talamadagi of Chitaguppa Taluk, Bidar district to form a new revenue
village called as Gandhinagar of Chitaguppa Taluk, Bidar district along with the
survey numbers included in column (4) thereof.

PR-1057
GOVERNEMNT OF KARNATAKA
RD 942 Kamgrara 2017 Karnataka Government Secretariat
Multi Storied Building
Bengaluru, Date: 05-11-2022

FINAL NOTIFICATION
Whereas the draft of the Notification No. RD 942 Kamgrara 2017 Dated:22-
12-2017 regarding altering the limits of Yaladabagi village of Sira Taluk and
Tumkuru District and constituting a new revenue village called Kuntegowdanahalli,
Havinalu and Handechikkanahalli was published as required by section 6 of the
Karnataka Land Revenue Act, 1964 (Karnataka act No.12 of 1964) in the Karnataka
Gazette No. RD 942 Kamgrara 2017 Dated:08-02-2018 inviting objections or
suggestions from all the persons likely to be affected thereby within thirty days from
the date of its publication in the Official Gazette.
And whereas, the said gazette was made available to the public on
Dated:14-02-2018
And whereas, objections and suggestions have been received and considered by
the State Government;
Now, therefore, in exercise of the powers conferred by section 5 of Karnataka Land
Revenue Act, 1964 (Karnataka act No.12 of 1964) the Government of Karnataka here
by makes the following notification, namely:-

SCHEDULE-1

Land bearing survey numbers of the village and survey numbers of the areas
specified in column (2) of the table below shall be excluded from the limits of
Yaladabagi said village of Sira taluk in Tumkuru District to form a new revenue

village called Kuntegowdanahalli of Sira taluk Tumkuru district along with survey
numbers included in column (4) thereof.

PR-1058

GOVERNEMNT OF KARNATAKA

RD 210 PFH 2022 Karnataka Government Secretariat
Multi Storied Building,
Bengaluru, Date:05-11-2022

PRELIMINARY NOTIFICATION
In exercise of the powers conferred by section 5 of the Karnataka Land Revenue
Act, 1964 (Karnataka Act 12 of 1964) and taking into consideration the factors
specified in clauses (i) to (iv) of rule 3 of the Karnataka Land Revenue Rules 1966
such as the population, area under land revenue and having regard to the administrative
convenience, the Government of Karnataka proposes to after, the extent of residential
area by adding an area occupied by unrecorded habitation within the boundary of
Ankalakoppa Village of Gubbi Taluk of Tumkuru District to convert the existing
Veerannanagudi unrecorded habitation in to a hamlet/extension /part of the existing
revenue village. The area occupied by the said hamlet of the village is specified in the
schedule below.
Notification is hereby issued that Land bearing survey numbers and extent
specified in Schedule shall be declared as habitation area within the boundary of
Ankalakoppa village for conversion of Veerannanagudi unrecorded habitation in to
hamlet/extension/ part of the said revenue village.
Now therefore, in exercise of the powers conferred by Section 6 of the said Act,
notice is hereby given for the information of all the person likely to be affected thereby
that the proposal will be taken into consideration after thirty days from the date of
publication of the notification in the official Gazette.
Any objection or suggestion which may be received by the State Government
from any persons with respect to the said proposal before the expiry of the period
specified above will be considered by the State Government Objections or suggestions
may be addressed to the Deputy Commissioner of Tumkuru District.
SCHEDULE
Land bearing survey numbers and extent specified below in column (3) of the
table shall be declared as habitation area of Veerannanagudi village of Gubbi Taluk
of Tumkuru District convert the same to form hamlet/extension within the boundary
of Ankalakoppa Revenue village and same will herein after be named as
Veerannanagudi of Ankalakoppa village in Gubbi Taluk of Tumkuru District.

PRELIMINARY NOTIFICATION

In exercise of the powers conferred by Section 5 of the Karnataka Land
Revenue Act 1964 (Karnataka act no 12 of 1964) and taking into consideration the
factors specified in clauses (i) to (iv) of rule 3 of the Karnataka Land Revenue Rules
1966 such as the population, area of the village the demand under land revenue in the
village and other head of the account and having regard to the administrative
convenience the Government of Karnataka proposes to issue a Notification to alter the
limits of Bhimanal Village of Kamalapur taluk Kalaburagi District and to
constitute a new revenue village called Manunayak nagar and Pandunayak nagar
as specified in schedule along with maps with old survey numbers and new assigned to
them.

Now, therefore in exercise of the power conferred by section 6 of the said Act,
notice is hereby given for the information of all the persons likely to be affected
thereby that the proposal will be taken into consideration on or after thirty days from
the date of publication of the notification in the official Gazette.

Any objection or suggestion which may be received by the state Government
from any person with respect to the said proposal before the date specified above will
be considered by the state Government. Objections or suggestion may be addressed to
the Deputy Commissioner, Kalburgi District, Kalaburagi

boundary of Bhimanal Revenue village and same will herein after be named as
Manunayak nagar of Bhimanal village in Kamalapur Taluk of Kalaburagi

village called Ghama Tanda as specified in Schedule along with maps with old

date of publication of the Notification in the official Gazette.

Any objection or suggestion which may be received by the state Government

PR-1062

GOVERNEMNT OF KARNATAKA

RD 211 PFH 2022 Karnataka Government Secretariat
Multi Storied Building,
Bengaluru, Date:05-11-2022

PRELIMINARY NOTIFICATION

In exercise of the powers conferred by section 5 of the Karnataka Land Revenue
Act, 1964 (Karnataka Act 12 of 1964) and taking into consideration the factors
specified in clauses (i) to (iv) of rule 3 of the Karnataka Land Revenue Rules 1966
such as the population, area under land revenue and having regard to the administrative
convenience, the Government of Karnataka proposes to after, the extent of residential
area by adding an area occupied by unrecorded habitation within the boundary of
Gowripur Village of Gubbi Taluk of Tumkuru District to convert the existing
Muttayyanahatti unrecorded habitation in to a hamlet/extension /part of the existing
revenue village. The area occupied by the said hamlet of the village is specified in the
schedule below.

Notification is hereby issued that Land bearing survey numbers and extent
specified in Schedule shall be declared as habitation area within the boundary of
Gowripur village for conversion of Muttayyanahatti unrecorded habitation in to
hamlet/extension/ part of the said revenue village.

Now therefore, in exercise of the powers conferred by Section 6 of the said Act,
notice is hereby given for the information of all the person likely to be affected thereby
that the proposal will be taken into consideration after thirty days from the date of
publication of the notification in the official Gazette.

Any objection or suggestion which may be received by the State Government
from any persons with respect to the said proposal before the expiry of the period
specified above will be considered by the State Government Objections or suggestions
may be addressed to the Deputy Commissioner of Tumkuru District.
SCHEDULE
Land bearing survey numbers and extent specified below in column (3) of the
table shall be declared as habitation area of Muttayyanahatti village of Gubbi Taluk
of Tumkuru District convert the same to form hamlet/extension within the boundary
of Gowripur Revenue village and same will herein after be named as
Muttayyanahatti of Gowripur village in Gubbi Taluk of Tumkuru District.

is located is proposed to be converted as habitation area (Gramatana) of
Muttayyanahatti hamlet/ extension/part of Gowripur village in Gubbi Taluk of
Tumkuru District.

The proposal specified above shall come into effect from such date as the State
Government may specify by Notification proposed to be issued under section 5 of the
Karnataka Land Revenue Act, 1964 (Karnataka Act No.12 of 1964).

PR-1063
GOVERNMENT OF KARNATAKA
RD 317 PFH 2022 Karnataka Government Secretariat
Multi Stored Building
Bengaluru Date:05-11-2022

PRELIMINARY NOTIFICATION
In exercise of the powers conferred by Section 5 of the Karnataka land Revenue
Act,1964(Karnataka Act No.12 of 1964) and taking into consideration the factors

specified in clauses (i) to (iv) of rule 3 of Karnataka Land Revenue Rules 1966 such as
the population, Area under land revenue and having regard to the administrative
convenience .The Government of Karnataka proposes issue a notification to alter the
limits of Ghataboral village of Humanabad Taluk, Bidar district to constitute a
new village called Jamanagar as specified in Schedule along with maps with old
survey and new survey numbers assigned to them.

Now therefore in exercise of the power conferred by section 6 of the said Act,
notice is hereby given for the information of tall the persons likely to be affected
thereby that the proposal will be taken consideration on or after thirty days from the
date of publication of the Notification in the official Gazette.

Any objection or suggestion which may be received by the state Government
from any person with respect to the said proposal before the date specified above will
be considered by the state Government .Objections or suggestions may be addressed to
the Deputy Commissioner Bidar District Bidar.

SCHEDULE-I

Lands bearing Survey numbers of the villages and survey numbers of the areas
specified in column (2) of the table below shall be excluded from the limits of said
villages Ghataboral of Humanabad Taluk in Bidar District to from new revenue
village called as Jamanagar of Humanabad Taluk and Bidar District along with the
survey numbers included in column (4) thereof.

PR-1064
GOVERNMENT OF KARNATAKA

RD 197 GLH 2022 Karnataka Government Secretariat
Multi storied Building
Bengaluru, Date:05/11/2022

PRELIMINARY NOTIFICATION
In exercise of the powers conferred by section 5 of the Karnataka Land Revenue
Act, 1964 (Karnataka Act 12 of 1964) and taking into consideration the factors
specified in clauses (i) to (iv) of rule 3 of the Karnataka Land Revenue Rules 1966
such as the population, area under land revenue and having regard to the administrative

convenience, the Government of Karnataka proposes to after, the extent of residential
area by adding an area occupied by unrecorded habitation within the boundary of
Mashal Village of Afzalapur Taluk of Kalaburagi District to convert the existing
Bingoli, Bingoli Bhavani Nagar Tanda, Hydra Tanda, Rajivnagar, Alagi Tanda
and Balu Tanda unrecorded habitation in to a hamlet/extension /part of the existing
revenue village. The area occupied by the said hamlet of the village is specified in the
schedule below.
Notification is hereby issued that Land bearing survey numbers and extent
specified in Schedule shall be declared as habitation area within the boundary of
village for conversion of unrecorded habitation in to hamlet/extension/ part of the said
revenue village.
Now therefore, in exercise of the powers conferred by Section 6 of the said Act,
notice is hereby given for the information of all the person likely to be affected thereby
that the proposal will be taken into consideration after thirty days from the date of
publication of the notification in the official Gazette.
Any objection or suggestion which may be received by the State Government
from any persons with respect to the said proposal before the expiry of the period
specified above will be considered by the State Government Objections or suggestions
may be addressed to the Deputy Commissioner of Kalaburagi District, Kalaburagi.

SCHEDULE-1

Land bearing survey numbers of the village and survey numbers of the areas
specified in column (2) of the table below shall be excluded from the limits of said
village Mashal of Afzalapur taluk in Kalaburagi District to form a new revenue
village called Bingoli of Afzalapur taluk in Kalaburagi district along with the survey
numbers included in column (4) thereof.

PR-1065
GOVERNEMNT OF KARNATAKA
RD 209 PFH 2022 Karnataka Government Secretariat
Multi Storied Building,
Bengaluru, Date:05-11--2022

PRELIMINARY NOTIFICATION
In exercise of the powers conferred by section 5 of the Karnataka Land Revenue
Act, 1964 (Karnataka Act 12 of 1964) and taking into consideration the factors
specified in clauses (i) to (iv) of rule 3 of the Karnataka Land Revenue Rules 1966
such as the population, area under land revenue and having regard to the administrative
convenience, the Government of Karnataka proposes to after, the extent of residential
area by adding an area occupied by unrecorded habitation within the boundary of
Shakunithimmanahalli Village of Koratagere Taluk of Tumkuru District to convert
the existing Kallipalya unrecorded habitation in to a hamlet/extension /part of the
existing revenue village. The area occupied by the said hamlet of the village is
specified in the schedule below.
Notification is hereby issued that Land bearing survey numbers and extent
specified in Schedule shall be declared as habitation area within the boundary of
Shakunithimmanahalli village for conversion of Kallipalya unrecorded habitation in
to hamlet/extension/ part of the said revenue village.
Now therefore, in exercise of the powers conferred by Section 6 of the said Act,
notice is hereby given for the information of all the person likely to be affected thereby
that the proposal will be taken into consideration after thirty days from the date of
publication of the notification in the official Gazette.
Any objection or suggestion which may be received by the State Government
from any persons with respect to the said proposal before the expiry of the period
specified above will be considered by the State Government Objections or suggestions
may be addressed to the Deputy Commissioner of Tumkuru District.

SCHEDULE
Land bearing survey numbers and extent specified below in column (3) of the
table shall be declared as habitation area of Kallipalya village of Koratagere Taluk of
Tumkuru District convert the same to form hamlet/extension within the boundary of
Shakunithimmanahalli Revenue village and same will herein after be named as
Kallipalya of Shakunithimmanahalli village in Koratagere Taluk of Tumkur
District.

with survey numbers specified in column (3) on which the said unrecorded habitation
is located is proposed to be converted as habitation area (Gramatana) of Kallipalya
hamlet/ extension/part of Shakunithimmanahalli village in Koratagere Taluk of

Government may specify by Notification proposed to be issued under section 5 of the
Karnataka Land Revenue Act, 1964 (Karnataka Act No.12 of 1964).

PR-1066
GOVERNMENT OF KARNATAKA

RD 281 PFH 2022 Karnataka Government Secretariat
Multi storied Building
Bengaluru, Date:05/11/2022

PRELIMINARY NOTIFICATION
In exercise of the powers conferred by section 5 of the Karnataka Land Revenue
Act, 1964 (Karnataka Act 12 of 1964) and taking into consideration the factors

specified in clauses (i) to (iv) of rule 3 of the Karnataka Land Revenue Rules 1966
such as the population, area under land revenue and having regard to the administrative
convenience, the Government of Karnataka proposes to after, the extent of residential
area by adding an area occupied by unrecorded habitation within the boundary of
Sonta Village of Kamalapur Taluk of Kalaburagi District to convert the existing
Jotunayak nagar, Kevalunayak nagar, Purunayak nagar, Harjinayak nagar and
Basavanagar unrecorded habitation in to a hamlet/extension /part of the existing
revenue village. The area occupied by the said hamlet of the village is specified in the
schedule below.
Notification is hereby issued that Land bearing survey numbers and extent
specified in Schedule shall be declared as habitation area within the boundary of
village for conversion of unrecorded habitation in to hamlet/extension/ part of the said
revenue village.
Now therefore, in exercise of the powers conferred by Section 6 of the said Act,
notice is hereby given for the information of all the person likely to be affected thereby
that the proposal will be taken into consideration after thirty days from the date of
publication of the notification in the official Gazette.
Any objection or suggestion which may be received by the State Government
from any persons with respect to the said proposal before the expiry of the period
specified above will be considered by the State Government Objections or suggestions
may be addressed to the Deputy Commissioner of Kalaburagi District, Kalaburagi.

SCHEDULE-1

Land bearing survey numbers of the village and survey numbers of the areas
specified in column (2) of the table below shall be excluded from the limits of said
village Sonta of Kamalapur taluk in Kalaburagi District to form a new revenue
village called Jotunayak nagar of Kamalapur taluk in Kalaburagi district along
with the survey numbers included in column (4) thereof.

Whereas the draft of the Notification No. RD 136 PFH 2022 Dated:24.06.2022
regarding altering the limits of Atharga village of Indi Taluk and Vijayapur District
and constituting a new revenue village called Ramatheerthnagar & Padagaanuru
was published as required by section 6 of the Karnataka Land Revenue Act, 1964
(Karnataka act No.12 of 1964) in the Karanataka Gazette No. RD 136 PFH 2022
dated:27.07.2022 inviting objections or suggestions from all the persons likely to be
affected thereby within thirty days from the date of its publication in the Official
Gazette.
And whereas, the said gazette was made available to the public on Dated:
27.07.2022
And whereas, objections and suggestions have been received and considered by the
State Government;
Now, therefore, in exercise of the powers conferred by section 5 of Karnataka Land
Revenue Act, 1964 (Karnataka act No.12 of 1964) the Government of Karnataka here
by makes the following notification, namely:

SCHEDULE-I
Land bearing survey numbers of the villages and survey numbers of the areas
specified in column (2) of the table below shall be excluded from the limits of said
Atharga village of Indi taluk ,Vijayapur District to form a new revenue village called
as Ramatheerthnagar of Indi taluk, Vijayapura District along with the survey
numbers included in column (4) thereof.

Multi Storied Building
Bengaluru, Date:05-11-2022

FINAL NOTIFICATION
Whereas the draft of the Notification No.RD 1026 KAMGRARA 2017 Dated :
03-08-2018 regarding altering the limits of Hitnalli village of Devara Hippargi
Taluk and Vijayapura District for constituting a new revenue village called
Chandanagara was published as required by section 6 of the Karnataka Land
Revenue Act, 1964 (Karnataka act No.12 of 1964) in the Karanataka Gazette No. RD
1026 KAMGRARA 2017 dated:13-09-2018 inviting objections or suggestions from
all the persons likely to be affected thereby within thirty days from the date of its
publication in the Official Gazette.
And whereas, the said gazette was made available to the public on Dated:06-09-
And whereas, objections and suggestions have been received and considered by the
State Government;
Now, therefore, in exercise of the powers conferred by section 5 of Karnataka Land
Revenue Act, 1964 (Karnataka act No.12 of 1964) the Government of Karnataka here
by makes the following notification, namely:-

SCHEDULE-1

Land bearing survey numbers of the village and survey numbers of the areas
specified in column (2) of the table below shall be excluded from the limits of said
village of Devara Hippargi taluk in Vijayapura District to form a new revenue
village called Chandanagara of Devara Hippargi taluk Vijayapura district along
with survey numbers included in column (4) thereof.

PR-1069
GOVERNMENT OF KARNATAKA
RD 329 PFH 2022 Karnataka Government Secretariat
Multi Storied Building
Bangalore, Date:05-11-2022

PRELIMINARY NOTIFICATION
In exercise of the powers conferred by Section 5 of the Karnataka Land Revenue
Act 1964 (Karnataka act no 12 of 1964) and taking into consideration the factors
specified in clauses (i) to (iv) of rule 3 of the Karnataka Land Revenue Rules 1966
such as the population, area of the village the demand under land revenue in the village
and other head of the account and having regard to the administrative convenience the
Government of Karnataka proposes to issue a Notification to alter the limits of
Ashihal Village of Lingasugur taluk Raichur District and to constitute a new revenue
village called Vidyanagar as specified in schedule along with maps with old
survey numbers and new assigned to them.

Now, therefore in exercise of the power conferred by section 6 of the said Act,
notice is hereby given for the information of all the persons likely to be affected
thereby that the proposal will be taken into consideration on or after thirty days from
the date of publication of the notification in the official Gazette.

Any objection or suggestion which may be received by the state Government
from any person with respect to the said proposal before the date specified above will
be considered by the state Government. Objections or suggestion may be addressed to
the Deputy Commissioner, Raichur District, Raichur

SCHEDULE- 1

Land bearing survey numbers of the village and survey numbers of the areas
specified in column (2) of the table below shall be excluded from the limits of said
village Ashihal of Lingasugur taluk in Raichur District to form a new revenue
village called Vidyanagar of Lingasugur taluk Raichur district along with the
survey numbers included in column (4) thereof.

PRELIMINARY NOTIFICATION
In exercise of the powers conferred by Section 5 of the Karnataka Land
Revenue Act. 1964 (Karnataka Act No. 12 of 1964) and taking in to consideration the
factors specified in clauses (i) to (iv) of rule 3 of the Karnataka Land revenue Rules
1966 such as the population, area of the village, the demand under land revenue in the
village and having regard to the administrative convenience the government of
Karnataka proposes to issue a notification to alter the limits of Bagalawada village of
Sirwar Taluk, Raichur District and to constitute a new village called Suryanagar as
specified in schedule along with maps with old survey numbers and new survey
numbers assigned to them.
Now therefore in exercise of the power conferred by Section 6 of the said, act,
Notice is hereby given for the information of all the persons likely to be affected
thereby that the proposal will be taken in to consideration on or after 30 days from the
date of publication of the consideration in the official Gazette.

Any objection or suggestion which may be received by the State Government
from any person with respect to the said proposal before the date specified above will
be considered by the State Government. Objections or suggestions may be addressed
to the Deputy Commissioner, Raichur District, Raichur.

SCHEDULE-1
Land bearing Survey numbers of the village and survey numbers of the areas
specified in column (2) of the table below shall be excluded from the limits of said
village Bagalawada of Sirwar Taluk in Raichur District to form a new revenue
Village Called as Suryanagar of Sirwar Taluk and Raichur District along with the
survey numbers included in column (4) therof.

PR-1071
GOVERNMENT OF KARNATAKA
RD 24 PFH 2022 Karnataka Government Secretariat
Multi Storied Building
Bangalore, Date:05-11-2022

FINAL NOTIFICATION
Whereas the draft of the notification No. RD 24 PFH 2022
Dated: 04-04-2022 regarding altering the extent of habitation area in the limits of
Anupanahalli village of Sira Taluk in Tumkuru District for constituting
hamlet/extention called Anupanahalli Tanda within the boundary of Anupanahalli
revenue village was published as required by section 6 of Karnataka Land Revenue
Act, 1964 (Karnataka Act 12 of 1964) in the Karnataka Gazette No.
RD 24 PFH 2022 Dated: 17-04-2022 inviting objections or suggestions from all the
persons likely to be affected thereby within thirty days from the date of its publication
in the Official Gazette.

And whereas, the said Gazette was made available to public on
Dated: 19-04-2022 and whereas, objections and suggestions have been received and
considered by the State Government;

Now, therefore in exercise of the powers conferred by section 5 of the
Karnataka Land Revenue At, 1964 (Karnataka Act No.12 of 1964) the Government of
Karnataka hereby makes the following notification to constitute a hamlet/extention
within the limits of Anupanahalli revenue village.

SCHEDULE-1

Lands bearing survey numbers and extent specified below in column(3) of the
table shall be declared as habitation area of Anupanahalli Tanda village of Sira
Taluk in Tumkuru District and convert the same to form hamlet/extention within the
boundary of Anupanahalli Revenue village and same will herein after be named as of
Anupanahalli Tanda village Sira Taluk in Tumkuru District.

is located is proposed to be converted as habitation area(Gramatana) of Anupanahalli

The proposal specified above Shall come in to effect from the date of final
notification to be published in the official Gazette by the State Government under
section 5 of the Karnataka Land Revenue Act, 1964 (Karnataka Act 12 of 1964).

FINAL NOTIFICATION
Whereas the draft of the Notification No. RD 157 PFH 2022 Dated:10-
08-2022 regarding altering the limits of Bevinahalli village of Sira Taluk and
Tumkuru District and constituting a new revenue villages called Bommaganahalli,
Shivayyanapalya and Brahmasandra was published as required by section 6 of the
Karnataka Land Revenue Act, 1964 (Karnataka act No.12 of 1964) in the Karanataka
Gazette No. RD 157 PFH 2022 Dated:18-08-2022 inviting objections or
suggestions from all the persons likely to be affected thereby within thirty days from
the date of its publication in the Official Gazette.
And whereas, the said gazette was made available to the public on
Dated:26-08-2022 And whereas, objections and suggestions have been received and
considered by the State Government;
Now, therefore, in exercise of the powers conferred by section 5 of Karnataka
Land Revenue Act, 1964 (Karnataka act No.12 of 1964) the Government of Karnataka
here by makes the following notification, namely:-

SCHEDULE-1

Land bearing survey numbers of the village and survey numbers of the areas
specified in column (2) of the table below shall be excluded from the limits of said
village Bevinahalli of Sira Taluk and Tumkuru District to form a new revenue
village called Bommaganahalli of Sira Taluk in Tumkuru district along with
survey numbers included in column (4) thereof.

PR-1073
GOVERNEMNT OF KARNATAKA
Rd 659 Kamgrara 2017 Karnataka Government Secretariat
Multi Storied Building
Bengaluru, Date:05/11/2022

FINAL NOTIFICATION
Whereas the draft of the Notification No. RD 659 Kamgrara 2017 Dated:06-
09-2017 regarding altering the limits of Guligenahalli village of Sira Taluk and
Tumkuru District and constituting a new revenue village called Javanahalli was
published as required by section 6 of the Karnataka Land Revenue Act, 1964
(Karnataka act No.12 of 1964) in the Karnataka Gazette No. RD 659 Kamgrara 2017
Dated:12-10-2017 inviting objections or suggestions from all the persons likely to be
affected thereby within thirty days from the date of its publication in the Official
Gazette.
And whereas, the said gazette was made available to the public on
Dated:17-10-2017 And whereas, objections and suggestions have been received and
considered by the State Government;
Now, therefore, in exercise of the powers conferred by section 5 of Karnataka
Land Revenue Act, 1964 (Karnataka act No.12 of 1964) the Government of Karnataka
here by makes the following notification, namely:-

SCHEDULE-1
Land bearing survey numbers of the village and survey numbers of the areas
specified in column (2) of the table below shall be excluded from the limits of said
village Guligenahalli of Sira Taluk and Tumkuru District to form a new revenue
village called Javanahalli of Sira Taluk in Tumkuru district along with survey
numbers included in column (4) thereof.

which are excluded transferred to new in column (2) which are to
from village be included to form new
Guligenahalli village to Javanahalli village Javanahalli new
constituting new Acre Gunta Anna survey numbers assigned
village Javanahalli to them

Multi Storied Building
Bengaluru, Date: 05/11/2022

FINAL NOTIFICATION
Whereas the draft of the Notification No RD 653 Kamgrara 2017 Dated:16-
08-2017 regarding altering the limits of Kodigihalli village of Gubbi Taluk and
Tumkuru District and constituting a new revenue village called Kodigihalli Palya
was published as required by section 6 of the Karnataka Land Revenue Act, 1964
(Karnataka act No.12 of 1964) in the Karnataka Gazette No. RD 653 Kamgrara 2017
Dated:14-09-2017 inviting objections or suggestions from all the persons likely to be
affected thereby within thirty days from the date of its publication in the Official
Gazette.

And whereas, the said gazette was made available to the public on Dated:12-
12-2017 And whereas, objections and suggestions have been received and considered
by the State Government;

Now, therefore, in exercise of the powers conferred by section 5 of Karnataka
Land Revenue Act, 1964 (Karnataka act No.12 of 1964) the Government of Karnataka
here by makes the following notification, namely:-

SCHEDULE-1
Land bearing survey numbers of the village specified in column (2) of the table
below and areas specified in column (3) there of shall be excluded from the limits of
said village Kodigihalli of Gubbi Taluk and Tumkuru District to form a new
revenue village called Kodigihalli Palya of Gubbi aluk in Tumkuru district along
with survey numbers included in column (4) thereof.

GOVERNMENT OF KARNATAKA

RD 25 PFH 2022 Karnataka Government Secretariat
Multi Storied Building
Bangalore, Date:05/11/2022

FINAL NOTIFICATION
Whereas the draft of the notification No. RD 25 PFH 2022
Dated: 12-04-2022 regarding altering the extent of habitation area in the limits of
Manangi village of Sira Taluk in Tumkuru District for constituting hamlet/extention
called Manangi Tanda within the boundary of Manangi revenue village was
published as required by section 6 of Karnataka Land Revenue Act, 1964 (Karnataka
Act 12 of 1964) in the Karnataka Gazette No. RD 25 PFH 2022 Dated: 12-04-2022
inviting objections or suggestions from all the persons likely to be affected thereby
within thirty days from the date of its publication in the Official Gazette.

And whereas, the said Gazette was made available to public on
Dated: 19-04-2022 and whereas, objections and suggestions have been received and
considered by the State Government;

Now, therefore in exercise of the powers conferred by section 5 of the
Karnataka Land Revenue At, 1964 (Karnataka Act No.12 of 1964) the Government of
Karnataka hereby makes the following notification to constitute a hamlet/extention
within the limits of Manangi revenue village.

SCHEDULE-1

Lands bearing survey numbers and extent specified below in column(3) of the
table shall be declared as habitation area of Manangi Tanda village of Sira Taluk in
Tumkuru District and convert the same to form hamlet/extention within the boundary
of Manangi Revenue village and same will herein after be named as of Manangi
Tanda village Sira Taluk in Tumkuru District.

GOVERNMENT OF KARNATAKA

RD 63 PFH 2022 Karnataka Governament Secretariat
Multi Storied Building
Bangaluru,Date:05-11-2022

PRELIMINARY NOTIFICATION

In exercise of the powers conferred by Section 5 of the Karnataka Land Revenue
Act, 1964 (Karnataka act no 12 of 1964) and taking into consideration the factors
specified in clauses (i) to (iv)of rule 3 of the Karnataka Land Revenue Rules 1966
such as the population , area of the village the demand under land revenue in the
village and having regard to the administrative convenience the Government of
Karnataka proposes to issue a Notification to alter the limits of K. Shankaranahalli
village of Arasikere Taluk, Hassan district and to constitute a new village called

Shivanahalli of Arasikere Taluk, Hassan district as specified in schedule along with
maps with old survey numbers and new survey numbers assigned to them.

Now therefore in exercise of the power conferred by section 6 of the said Act
notice is hereby given for the information of all the persons likely to be affected
thereby that the proposal will be taken in to consideration on or after thirty days from
the date of publication of the notification in the official Gazette.

Any objection or suggestion which may be received by the state government
from any person with respect to the said proposal before the date specified above will
be considered by the state government. Objections or suggestions may be addressed to
the Deputy commissioner, Hassan district Hassan.

SCHEDULE-1
Lands bearing survey numbers of the village and survey numbers of the areas
specificd in column (2) of the table below shall be excluded from the limits of said
K.Shankaranahalli village of Arasikere Taluk, Hassan Disrict to form a new
revenue village called Shivanahalli of Arasikere Taluk, Hassan Disrict along with
the survey numbers included in colum (4) there of.

PR-1078
GOVERNEMNT OF KARNATAKA
RD18 GLH 2022 Karnataka Government Secretariat
Multi Storied Building
Bengaluru, Date:05/11/2022

FINAL NOTIFICATION
Whereas the draft of the Notification No RD 18 GLH 2022 Dated:01-02-
2022 regarding altering the limits of Teggi village of Bilagi Taluk and Bagalakote
District and constituting a new revenue village called Shivapura was published as
required by section 6 of the Karnataka Land Revenue Act, 1964 (Karnataka act No.12
of 1964) in the Karanataka Gazette No. RD 18 GLH 2022 Dated:05-02-2022 inviting
objections or suggestions from all the persons likely to be affected thereby within
thirty days from the date of its publication in the Official Gazette.
And whereas, the said gazette was made available to the public on
Dated:04-04-2022
And whereas, objections and suggestions have been received and considered by
the State Government;
Now, therefore, in exercise of the powers conferred by section 5 of Karnataka
Land Revenue Act, 1964 (Karnataka act No.12 of 1964) the Government of Karnataka
here by makes the following notification, namely:-

SCHEDULE-1

Land bearing survey numbers of the village and survey numbers of the areas
specified in column (2) of the table below shall be excluded from the limits of said
village Teggi of Bilagi taluk in Bagalakote District to form a new revenue village
called Shivapura of Bilagi taluk Bagalakote district along with survey numbers
included in column (4) thereof.
